## trait_infernal_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_infernal_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该物种进化出由金属合金构成的组织，能在传统有机体会被烧煮的高温行星上繁衍生息。这些合金细胞退化速度慢，更换频率低，延缓了与年龄相关疾病的发生。但此类细胞还会传导低温，使该物种在寒冷气候中易受威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:12`

### English

This species evolved tissues of metallic alloys to grow and thrive on hot planets where traditional organics would cook. These alloyed cells are slower to degrade and are replaced infrequently, delaying the onset of age-related diseases. They also conduct cold temperatures, which can threaten their health in colder climates.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:14`

## trait_unbreakable_resolve_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unbreakable_resolve_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

每他们依托复杂的镜像神经网络彼此相连，天生拥有同步意志。责任对他们而言不是负担，而是一种共有的使命；面对困难，他们总能同心而行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:15`

### English

Bound by an intricate web of mirror neurons, these individuals possess an innate ability to synchronize their will. Duty is not a burden, but a shared purpose, and hardship is met with unwavering unity.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:18`

## trait_unbreakable_resolve_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unbreakable_resolve_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

该特性所在行星上的每$pop_group_unit$£pop£§H个人口§!增加§G+0.2§!£stability£$PLANET_STABILITY_TITLE$（上限：§G+15§!）\n—§R不包括奴隶或较低人口阶层。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:16`

### English

Every $pop_group_unit$ £pop£ §HPops§! on the planet with this trait add §G+0.2§! £stability£ $PLANET_STABILITY_TITLE$ (Max: §G+15§!) \n- §RDoes not include slaves or lower pop strata.§!

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:19`

## trait_shell_slag_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_shell_slag_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

与$r_living_metal$的复杂共生使该物种的生长不同于传统方式，而是通过生物铁结构的累积与自组装实现成长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:18`

### English

An intricate symbiosis with $r_living_metal$ allows this species to grow not in the traditional sense, but through the accretion and self-assembly of bio-ferrous structures.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:22`

## trait_shell_slag_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_shell_slag_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

该特性下作为奴隶或更高身份工作的每个$pop_group_unit$£pop£§H人口§!每月生产§G$@shell_slag_trait_resource_production|3$§!§Y$r_living_metal$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:19`

### English

Every $pop_group_unit$ £pop£ §HPops§! with this trait working as a slave or better produces §G$@shell_slag_trait_resource_production|3$§! §Y$r_living_metal$§! per month.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:23`

## trait_robot_assembly_slag_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_robot_assembly_slag_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$r_living_metal$的复杂整合使该机械种族不依靠传统建筑，而是通过自动铁框架的累积和自组装扩展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:21`

### English

### English

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:27`

## trait_pyroclastic_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_pyroclastic_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这一物种诞生于火山星球的炽烈炼狱，早已习惯与灾难为伴。他们不惧那席卷大地的熔岩碎屑流，反而学会驯服与引导其狂怒，将毁灭之力淬炼为生机与能量的源泉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

Shaped by the relentless fury of volcanic worlds, this species does not fear pyroclastic forces - they channel and manipulate them, transforming devastation into a vital source of sustenance and power.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:28`

## trait_crucible_community_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_crucible_community_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该物种经过逆境锤炼并以共同奋斗为纽带，成为坚不可摧的整体。集体意志推动治理，每个成员是链条中的关键环节。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:26`

### English

Tempered by adversity and bound by shared struggle, this species has been forged into an unbreakable whole. Their collective will fuels governance, each member a vital link in the chain of unity.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:32`

## pc_volcanic_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apc_volcanic_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

岩石圈仍处于半熔融状态，硫磺浓重的大气笼罩其上。地表布满火山灰与玄武岩，活火山频繁喷发，熔岩湖与熔岩河纵横交错。若有生命存在，也只能是极端环境下的幸存者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:33`

### English

Rocky world with a sulfur rich atmosphere and a partially liquid lithosphere. The ash and basalt covered surface is interspersed with fresh volcanic activity, lava lakes and rivers. Only extremophile life forms can be found, if any.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:41`

## origin_red_giant_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_red_giant_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个文明正站在命运的十字路口：其恒星系中央的红巨星正在持续升温，炙烤着他们的家园。\n\n在找到原因并解决之前，他们是否能适应这恶劣的环境？或选择拥抱这股炙热之力，将那诡异、甚至可能不自然的变化化为己用？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:84`

### English

This civilization is at a crossroads: the Red Giant at the heart of its system is warming up their planet, threatening to cook them alive.\n\nCan they adapt until the cause is found and fixed, or will they decide to embrace the heat, capitalizing on these strange and perhaps unnatural changes?

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:100`

## origin_tooltip_red_giant_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_tooltip_red_giant_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

—$HOMEWORLD$['concept_pc_volcanic']（规模：£planetsize£20）

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:85`

### English

- $HOMEWORLD$ ['concept_pc_volcanic'] (Size: £planetsize£ 20)

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:101`

## START_SCREEN_RED_GIANT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_RED_GIANT&lang=en)

### 中文

真相来得缓慢，却又骤然明了。\n\n我们的恒星正进入一个快速且异常的演变阶段。其能量输出已超出所有预期参数。光谱转变显示异常对流模式，其核聚变速率似乎在无相应质量损失情况下提升。\n\n迄今为止，我们与这股升温同步演化。但基础生物进化有其极限。\n\n若[capital_star.GetName]内正在进行的变化持续，母星将被焚毁。问题不再是“是否”，而是“何时”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

The truth came slowly, then all at once.\n\nOur star is entering a phase of rapid and anomalous evolution. Its energy output has climbed beyond all expected parameters. Spectral shifts indicate erratic convection patterns, and its fusion rate appears to be increasing without a corresponding loss of mass.\n\nSo far, we have evolved in concert with this warming. But basic biological evolution has its limits.\n\nIf the changes underway within [capital_star.GetName] continue, the world we call home will burn. It is no longer a question of if, but when.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:102`

## tech_starfire_cannon_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_starfire_cannon_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如同任何潜在危害，我们恒星转变产生的危险辐射也能成为武器。将其引导为束能武器，可使敌舰过热瘫痪。\n\n解锁殖民地决议，在轨道上建造§Y$starfire_cannon$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

Like any potential hazard, the dangerous radiation from our star's transformation can also be harnessed as a useful tool. Channeling it as a beam weapon would allow us to overheat and disable enemy ships. \n\nUnlocks a Colony Decision to construct a §Y$starfire_cannon$§! in orbit.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:107`

## decision_starfire_cannon_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_starfire_cannon_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

模拟迅速老化红巨星的强烈辐射，该轨道炮发射高温电离束，不仅能损伤目标，还会烧毁舰船组件使其失效。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

Mimicking the intense radiation of a rapidly aging red giant star, this orbital cannon projects a beam of hyperthermal ionization that not only damages its targets but also fries ship components to render them inoperable.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:119`

## decision_starfire_cannon_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_starfire_cannon_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

Builds a §Y$starfire_cannon$§! in the planet's orbit, a long-ranged weapon that can disable the systems of multiple ships.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:120`

## origin_cosmic_dawn_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_cosmic_dawn_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这不是一个崛起的文明，而是一个被释放的文明。当休眠仓开启，他们醒来时，早已物换星移，故土难寻。但他们的过去并未湮灭，而是埋藏在星系尘土与废墟之下，静候再次被发掘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

This civilization did not rise; it was released. As the stasis pods unlocked, they awakened to a world aged beyond recognition. Yet all is not lost. Scattered beneath dust and ruin across the galaxy, their past endures, waiting to be rediscovered.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:125`

## START_SCREEN_COSMIC_DAWN

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_COSMIC_DAWN&lang=en)

### 中文

我们苏醒之时，只能依稀感知自己曾经沉眠。即便如今，我们对往昔最确凿的证据，也并非记忆本身，而是那保存休眠躯体的庞大而精密的科技体系。\n\n成千上万的休眠舱，贯穿地下的传热系统，翻腾不息的熔岩之炉。墙壁上刻满了我们的起源痕迹：舰队自逐渐冷却的母星启程，奔赴群星之间，追寻新的温暖。为了寻找真相，我们离开了这座地下“孵化室”。在§Y[owner.Capital.GetName]§!的地表，我们找到了可用的科技与资源，也在行星地壳深处探测到更多隐藏的设施。我们的同族或许正在其中一些设施中安眠。\n\n那群星呢？是否在遥远的虚空中，还有如我们一般沉睡的族群？若真如此，也许他们能助我们解开那个自觉醒起便萦绕心头的疑问——我们是谁？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

We woke with only the faintest sense that we had ever fallen asleep. Even now, our strongest evidence of a time before comes not from our own memories, but from the complex technology that preserved us in stasis.\n\nThousands upon thousands of pods, massive heat-transfer systems, cauldrons churning molten rock. Beyond these bare mechanisms, clues to our origin were inscribed in the walls: fleets of ships, scattering from cooling worlds to seek warmth among the stars. In search of answers, we left this underground 'birthing chamber.' Across the surface of §Y[owner.Capital.GetName]§! we discovered useful technology and resources. We have also found evidence of further facilities nestled within the planet's crust. Some hold sleepers we might welcome as kin.\n\nAnd what of the stars? Are there others like us, still sleeping? If they exist, perhaps they can help us answer our most burning question: who are we?

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:128`

## d_stasis_pods_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_stasis_pods_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些休眠仓为失落亲族的远古设备，利用先进低温技术使个体暂停生命体征。借助特定科技或许能唤醒他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

These stasis pods are ancient devices from our lost kin, using advanced cryogenic technology to keep individuals in suspended animation. With the right technology, we may be able to awaken them.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:140`

### English

Scorched World Heralds

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:150`

## civic_scorched_earth_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_scorched_earth_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

热量渗透社会文化各方面，从传统礼仪到科技。他们视自身为星系之中的真正火焰霸主，向往仅适合嗜热生命存活的理想宇宙，而弱者只会拖累这一愿景的达成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:132`

### English

Heat permeates every facet of this society's culture, from traditions and rituals to technology itself. They see themselves as the galaxy's rightful fiery supremacists, viewing weaker species as mere obstacles on the path to an ideal universe, fit only for Thermophiles.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:151`

## civic_scorched_earth_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_scorched_earth_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

—只能与§Y$trait_infernal_plural$§!展开§Y外交§!。\n—无法进入§Y泛星系贸易市场§!。\n—对其他物种获得§Y“$casus_belli_cb_blaze$”§!宣战理由。\n—以§Y$bombardment_firestorm$§!姿态轰炸时获得£unity£§Y$unity$§!\n—以§Y$bombardment_seismic_bombing$§!姿态轰炸时在所轰炸的行星上生成特殊地块障碍。\n—非['trait:trait_infernal']物种限定为§Y奴隶§!或更差生活标准。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:133`

### English

- Cannot engage in §YDiplomacy§! except with §Y$trait_infernal_plural$§!.\n- Cannot access the §YGalactic Market§!.\n- Grants the §Y'$casus_belli_cb_blaze$'§! casus belli on other species.\n- Gains £unity£ §Y$unity$§! when bombarding with the §Y$bombardment_firestorm$§! bombardment stance.\n- Creates special blockers with the §Y$bombardment_seismic_bombing$§! bombardment stance on the planet below. \n- Non-['trait:trait_infernal'] Species are restricted to §YSlavery§! or worse.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:152`

## SCORCHED_HERALDS_ONLY_INF

### 中文

## civic_pyrolatry_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_pyrolatry_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

历经数代苦难淬炼，此社会信奉团结才能生存，生存才能团结。唯有火焰熔铸的艰苦奋斗，方能凝聚起足以忍受冷漠星系的坚韧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

Forged by generations of extreme hardship, this society believes in survival through unity, and unity through survival. Only fire-born struggle can bind them tightly enough to endure a cold, uncaring galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:186`

## civic_pyrolatry_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_pyrolatry_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

—['concept_fire_cult']派系会在游戏开始后不久形成。\n—['concept_fire_cult_jobs']岗位。\n—允许在$HABITABILITY$为§R$@inf_ember_legion_max_habitability|0%$§!或更低的星球上招募§Y$ember_legion$§! 陆军。\n—解锁§Y$decision_blazing_scourge$§!殖民地决议，该决议会生成£blocker£ ['concept_dec_blazing_scourge']。\n—与以下国策产生协同效应：$civic_superstitious_beliefs$、$civic_exalted_priesthood$、$civic_death_cult$、$civic_aristocratic_elite$和$civic_merchant_guilds$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

- The ['concept_fire_cult'] faction forms shortly after game start.\n- ['concept_fire_cult_jobs'] Jobs.\n- Allows the recruitment of §Y$ember_legion$§! armies on planets with $HABITABILITY$ §R$@inf_ember_legion_max_habitability|0%$§! or less.\n- Grants access to the §Y$decision_blazing_scourge$§! Colony Decision which creates £blocker£ ['concept_dec_blazing_scourge'].\n- Synergizes with Civics: $civic_superstitious_beliefs$, $civic_exalted_priesthood$, $civic_death_cult$, $civic_aristocratic_elite$, and $civic_merchant_guilds$.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:187`

## job_fire_dancer_desc

### 中文

火焰舞者是火焰与耐力的大师，表演灼烈仪式，令社区共享敬畏与激情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

## job_cremator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_cremator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

火葬者掌控圣火仪式，引导逝者穿过火焰迈向终极奥秘。被尊为此崇高通道的守护者，服务于死亡与超脱的交界。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

Cremators oversee the sacred rite of flame, guiding the departed through fire to their final mystery. Honored as custodians of this revered passage, they serve where death meets transcendence.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:206`

## job_fire_seer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_fire_seer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

火焰肆虐后的混乱只余焦炭与灰烬，而技艺高超的先知能从其中辨识符号。那些秘奥般的解读被奉为仪式核心，也成为统治的凭依。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:192`

### English

The destructive chaos of fire still leaves behind signs in char and ash that a skilled seer can interpret. These esoteric insights are put to use in ritual and rule alike.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:217`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_pyromancer_desc&lang=en)

## job_radiant_priest_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_radiant_priest_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为保持信仰之火燃烧，掌控社会的祭司阶层展现神圣火焰光芒，照亮追随者之路，净化社会邪恶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:228`

### English

To keep burning the flames of faith, this society's ruling priesthood embodies the radiant light of holy fire, lighting the way for their followers and cleansing evil from society.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:257`

## job_pyrocrat_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_pyrocrat_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为维护体制根基的精英，火焰贵族如生生不息的火焰一般统治国家，依靠劳工的奉献维系荣耀。

### English

An entrenched elite whose brilliance defines the state, Pyrocrats rule as living flames and are sustained by the labor of those who stoke their glory.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:267`

## job_ashbroker_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_ashbroker_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

火焰燃尽处，灰烬经纪人兴盛。这些废墟之上的投机者善于将毁灭转化为机遇，将灰烬化为资产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

Where fire consumes, Ashbrokers thrive. These profiteers of ruin are skilled in turning devastation into opportunity and ashes into assets.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:277`

### 中文

是时候真正拥抱火焰，检验我们的勇气了。在整个行星燃起火焰！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

It is time for us to truly embrace the flames and test our mettle. Set the whole planet on fire!

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:299`

## decision_blazing_scourge_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_blazing_scourge_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

为行星增加最高§Y5§!£blocker£§R$BLOCKER_DEPOSITS$§!：$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—每个逐渐§R降低§!§Y$pop_habitability$§!§R20%§!。$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—授予£unity£§Y$unity$§!与£mod_planet_amenities_add£§Y$PLANET_AMENITIES_TITLE$§!。$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—若技术已研究，部分会赋予$r_volatile_motes$和$r_exotic_gases$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

Adds up to §Y5§! £blocker£ §R$BLOCKER_DEPOSITS$§! to the planet: $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- Each gradually §Rdecreases§! the §Y$pop_habitability$§! by §R20%§!. $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- Grants £unity£ §Y$unity$§! and £mod_planet_amenities_add£ §Y$PLANET_AMENITIES_TITLE$§!.$TABBED_NEW_LINE$- Some grant $r_volatile_motes$ and $r_exotic_gases$ if the tech is researched.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:300`

## factions.1.desc.fire_cult

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactions.1.desc.fire_cult&lang=en)

### 中文

其成员倡导心灵主义价值，颂扬艰苦与忍耐。体能试炼在其社会中占重要地位。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

Their members promote spiritualist values, praising hardship and the ability to suffer through it. Physical trials play a significant role in their society.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:310`

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

## civic_galvanic_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_galvanic_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

—开局已研究£engineering_research£ ['technology:tech_mine_living_metal']。\n—母星拥有['concept_d_galvanic_living_metal']。\n—获得特殊法令：$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['concept_inf_lm_cs_edict']$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['concept_inf_lm_mgc_edict']。\n—解锁$r_living_metal$组件：$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['concept_inf_lm_armor']$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['concept_inf_lm_weapon']\n—解锁殖民地决议：§Y$decision_living_metal_distribution$§!。\n—可招募§Y$lm_imbued_army$§!军队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

- Starts with £engineering_research£ ['technology:tech_mine_living_metal'] already researched. \n- Homeworld has a ['concept_d_galvanic_living_metal'].\n- Gain access to special Edicts: $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['concept_inf_lm_cs_edict'] $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['concept_inf_lm_mgc_edict'] \n- Gives access to $r_living_metal$ components: $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['concept_inf_lm_armor'] $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['concept_inf_lm_weapon']\n- Has access to Colony Decision: §Y$decision_living_metal_distribution$§!. \n- Can recruit the §Y$lm_imbued_army$§! armies.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:351`

## concept_inf_lm_armor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_inf_lm_armor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

装甲组件加成包括：\n—$mod_weapon_type_energy_weapon_fire_rate_mult$：§G+2%§!，\n—$MOD_SHIP_HULL_REGEN_ADD_PERC$：§G+1%§!，\n-$MOD_SHIP_ARMOR_REGEN_ADD_PERC$提供§G+1%§!。\n\n增加了§Y$INF_Regen$§!版常见舰船装甲组件。其$r_alloys$成本被替换为$r_living_metal$。\n\n包含以下£engineering_research£科技解锁的组件：$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['technology:tech_ship_armor_1']$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['technology:tech_ship_armor_2']$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['technology:tech_ship_armor_3']$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['technology:tech_ship_armor_4']$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['technology:tech_ship_armor_5']

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

Bonuses per Armor Component:\n- $mod_weapon_type_energy_weapon_fire_rate_mult$: §G+2%§! \n- $MOD_SHIP_HULL_REGEN_ADD_PERC$: §G+1%§! \n- $MOD_SHIP_ARMOR_REGEN_ADD_PERC$: §G+1%§! \n\nAdds §Y$INF_Regen$§! versions of common armor components for ships. Their $r_alloys$ cost is replaced with $r_living_metal$. \n\nIncludes unlocked components of the following £engineering_research£ technologies: $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['technology:tech_ship_armor_1'] $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['technology:tech_ship_armor_2'] $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['technology:tech_ship_armor_3'] $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['technology:tech_ship_armor_4'] $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['technology:tech_ship_armor_5']

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:362`

## volcanic_oasis_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avolcanic_oasis_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一片小小的翠绿大陆，在高山保护中躲过了烈焰肆虐。独特的动植物与山外荒芜形成鲜明对比。

### English

## fractalized_entropy_category_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afractalized_entropy_category_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

探测器发现一奇异物体在小行星带中漂移。其来源未知，但轨迹稳定，表明可拦截检查。

### English

Sensors have picked up a strange object drifting through the asteroid field. Its origin is unknown, but its course is steady, suggesting it can be intercepted and examined.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:595`

## infernals.3205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.3205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

分形熵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

Fractalized Entropy

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:596`

## infernals.3205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.3205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]报告发现一个有棱角的刚性结构体悬浮于恒星稳定轨道中。该结构体由未知材料组成，表面具有类似晶体结构的棱面，成分无法分析。它保持固定方向，始终面向其恒星，抵抗所有外力操控或旋转。\n\n在一项受控实验中，调查船对该结构体发射集束脉冲，致其整齐裂开，分裂成两个对称半体，每个半体又各自重构为原结构体的小型复制体。后续脉冲产生相同效果：分裂、复制及稳定微缩。\n\n最后，众多微型结构体分散形成一片云，稳定漂浮于轨道上。每一块碎片都保留了原结构体的精确排列与几何特征，整体则还原了原始结构轮廓。\n\n此现象前所未有且无法解释。所有数据均已归档，待深入分析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

[Leader.GetName] reported the discovery of a rigid, angular construct suspended in stable stellar orbit. Composed of an unknown material and marked by faceted planes reminiscent of crystalline structures, the object resisted all compositional analysis. It remained locked in a fixed orientation, perpetually facing its parent star, and withstood all attempts at manipulation or rotation.\n\nDuring a controlled experiment, the survey vessel subjected the object to a concentrated energy pulse. The construct fractured cleanly, splitting into two symmetrical halves, each of which reconfigured into a smaller replica of the original. Subsequent pulses produced identical outcomes: division, replication, and consistent miniaturization.\n\nEventually, a diffuse cloud of miniature constructs stabilized in orbit. Each fragment retained the precise alignment and geometry of the progenitor, and the swarm as a whole mirrored the original structure's silhouette.\n\nThe phenomenon remains entirely without precedent or explanation. All collected telemetry has been archived for further analysis.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:597`

## from_the_ashes_site

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afrom_the_ashes_site&lang=en)

### 中文

烈焰灰烬

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

From The Ashes

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:601`

## from_the_ashes_site_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afrom_the_ashes_site_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Planet.GetName]表面炙热，不适宜生物生存。行星上明显的人工遗迹影像引起了[GetScientistPlural]的注意。考古调查或可揭示这里的居民和曾发生在他们身上的故事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

The surface of [Planet.GetName] is scorching hot, and hostile to biological life. Images of clearly artificial ruins have therefore caught the attention of [GetScientistPlural]. An archaeological investigation could reveal who was here, and what happened to them.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:602`

## infernals.2700.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2700.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遗落帝国

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

Forgotten Empire

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:603`

## infernals.2700.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2700.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]遗迹初看似由石头构成，但挖掘和分析表明，许多建筑由石化的类木材有机物构成。现今行星上已无该材料来源。小型文物由粗制金属、玻璃及类似的石化有机物组成，[GetScientistPlural]还发现了已灭绝本土生物的化石。\n\n该文明疑居于气候温和时期。他们是早已消失，还是目睹了巨变的尾声？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

While the ruins on [From.Planet.GetName] first appeared to be built of stone, excavation and analysis suggests that many of the structures in this lost city are made of a petrified organic material comparable to wood. No source for such materials exists on the planet today. Smaller artifacts are built of crude metals, glass, and similar petrified organics, and [GetScientistPlural] have also found fossils of native creatures that no longer exist. \n\nIt appears the civilization in question inhabited the planet at a time when its climate was much more temperate. Did they disappear long before the planet turned to its current state, or did they witness the violent transition in their final years?

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:604`

## infernals.2705.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2705.name&lang=en)

### 中文

末日见证

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

Witnesses of Demise

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:605`

## infernals.2705.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2705.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]城市废墟中挖掘出若干具有文化价值的场所，墙面饰有复杂纹样，疑为某种文字。最激动人心的是，挖掘队发现一个密布书架的地下房间，摆满刻有繁复铭文的青铜薄板。青铜作为存储介质极为低效，但这里似乎是个公共文本档案库。\n\n考古队正深入探索并开始破译文本。该文明的最终命运仍待揭晓。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

Excavation of the ruined city on [From.Planet.GetName] has revealed places of cultural importance, decorated with intricate patterns that may be a written language. Most excitingly, the dig teams have found an underground chamber full of shelves, each containing thin sheets of bronze etched with densely-packed script. Despite the inefficiency of bronze as a storage medium, this appears to be a communal archive of texts. \n\nTranslation attempts have begun as the archaeological teams continue to spread out through the city. The question of the final fate of these people remains open.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:606`

## infernals.2710.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2710.name&lang=en)

### 中文

厄运逼近

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

Incoming Doom

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:607`

## infernals.2710.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2710.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上的团队发现了一处阴森的地下墓穴，由纵横交错的挖掘坑道构成，壁龛里叠放着三到四层木乃伊化的外星遗骸。陪葬品反映出该文明的科技面貌：有粗糙的火器、简易的玻璃望远镜，以及精细复杂的纺织刺绣。\n\n墓穴的多个交叉口由本地已灭绝动物的雕像把守。这些生物有圆顶甲壳、四条矮腿和小而钝的脑袋；在保存良好的雕像中，它们蜷伏着以壳护身，抵御上方刻画的火焰。\n\n这件艺术品的真实意涵或许已随时间消逝，但它很可能反映出社会对烈焰厄运即将来临的预感。若能深入发掘，或许能澄清该文明当年所经历之事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

The team on [From.Planet.GetName] has encountered a somber catacomb. It consists of a network of dugout trenches, with mummified alien remains resting in cavities in the walls, stacked three or four individuals high. Burial goods give us a glimpse of their technological development, such as primitive firearms, simple glass telescopes, and complex textile embroidery. \n\nMany junctions within the catacomb are guarded by statues of extinct local fauna. These creatures have a round, domed shell, four squat legs, and a small blunt head. In better-preserved specimens, they are depicted hunching down, using their shell to protect them from flame-like carvings in the material above them. \n\nThe true meaning of this artwork may be lost to time, but it may depict this society's awareness of its impending, fiery doom. Further excavation of the ruins may clarify what they were experiencing.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:608`

## infernals.2715.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2715.name&lang=en)

### 中文

烈火终结

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

Blazing Downfall

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:609`

## infernals.2715.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2715.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对[From.Planet.GetName]城市废墟的深入探索证明，该文明经历了生态系统的全面崩溃，并察觉到烈焰吞噬的末日逼近。最初他们以纸张记录历史，后因火山灰常年飘落，转而使用青铜板保存记录。艺术作品中频繁出现动物躲避火焰的场景，显然是在尝试理解并描绘自身的灭亡。\n\n挖掘过程中还发现了一尊近乎完好的地下墓穴守卫雕像，其顶部雕刻的火焰散发出微弱的光芒，极具保护价值。\n\n除此之外，我们还发现了他们试图阻止火山活动的证据，根据现存的道路与被毁的运输车可知，当地居民曾将碎石运往最近的火山口，企图封堵火山喷发，但终究未能改变灭亡的命运。如今，这颗行星早已不再存在任何多细胞生命。\n\n此段历史警示我们，一旦遭遇超乎自身技术的难题，任何文明都岌岌可危。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

Extensive exploration of the ruined city on [From.Planet.GetName] proves that this civilization lived through the collapse of its ecosystem, and knew its final, fiery days were approaching. Paper-based records existed before hot volcanic ashfall forced them to switch to bronze tablets, and artistic depictions of animals hiding from flame record their attempts to reckon with their own end. \n\nThe excavation also uncovered a nearly perfect specimen of the catacomb guardian statues. The flames carved above it have an internal glow, making this a striking piece worthy of preservation. \n\nWe also found evidence of attempts to stop the volcanism. Roadways and broken carts demonstrate that the inhabitants transported rubble to the nearest volcanic caldera and dumped it in to try to plug the volcano. Unfortunately, they were unable to stop their fate. The planet is devoid of multicellular life today. \n\nThis history reminds us of the precarity of any civilization, should it encounter a problem more powerful than the tools it has at its disposal.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:610`

## inferno_temple_site_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainferno_temple_site_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

远离[Planet.GetName]人口中心的活跃火山山脉中，一座古庙破败残存。了解其历史或能更深入了解当地文化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:556`

### English

Far from the population centers of [Planet.GetName], in a range of active volcanic mountains, an ancient temple lies in ruins and disrepair. Understanding its history may grant us further insights into the planet's local culture.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:615`

### English

## infernals.2720.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2720.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

神庙上存有战斗痕迹，土壤中残存当地智慧生命遗骸。神庙似被焚毁后遗弃。安保团队注意到当地居民完全避开此地，视之为诅咒。\n\n在外墙保护下，火焰神庙内部得以保存。里面留有大量精致的壁画和雕刻，其中多次出现一圈卷曲的火焰图案，火焰中央则是一道漆黑裂隙。在一幅壁画中，一个双足生物从自己的四只眼睛中挖出一只抛向大地，形成火焰中央的图案，我们将其命名为“地狱之眼”。\n\n后续发掘将揭示此异象相关信仰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

The temple itself is scarred by signs of battle, and there are ancient remains of the local sentient species in the soil. It seems the temple was torched and abandoned. Security teams have noted that the locals avoid this entire area, believing it to be cursed. \n\nThe fire temple's walls protected its contents, including many intricate frescos and carvings. These feature a recurring motif of a circle of twisted fire, with a dark gash down its center. In one depiction, a bipedal figure plucks out one of four such eyes and casts it upon the world, creating this central motif. We are designating it the Infernal Eye. \n\nFurther excavations will reveal what beliefs surrounded this strange figure.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:617`

## infernals.2725.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2725.name&lang=en)

### 中文

幽瞳祭品

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

Ocular Offerings

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:618`

## infernals.2725.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2725.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对地狱之眼神庙的发掘显示大量祭献痕迹。神庙内有多处大型圆坑，圆坑中央设有平台，模仿地狱之眼及瞳孔。坑内积满灰烬与炭块，分子分析表明这些来自动物组织。\n\n尚不明确祭品为野生或家养动物，还是当地居民。\n\n特工已前往当前人口中心寻找相关证据，挖掘队继续探寻答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

Excavations of the temple of the Infernal Eye show evidence of many sacrifices. Large circular pits with platforms in the center, mimicking the Infernal Eye and its pupil, lie filled with layers of ash and charcoal. Molecular analysis reveals that the ash and charcoal comes from animal tissue. \n\nWhether these sacrifices were of wild or domestic animals, or indeed of the locals themselves, cannot be determined. \n\nAgents have been dispatched to current population centers in search of corollary evidence, while our excavators search for more answers.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:619`

### English

### English

## infernals.2730.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2730.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一扇沉重的石门挡住了进入神庙最深处的路。破门后，挖掘队发现一个圆形房间，房间中央的坑洞直通地下的天然岩浆池。岩浆发出橙色光芒映红墙壁，隐约映照出环绕整个奇怪房间的铭文。墙壁顶端一周布满明亮炽热的眼睛。\n\n坑边祭坛形似地狱之眼，上面散落着一具干枯的当地居民遗骸，疑为祭献安抚地狱之眼所用。\n\n墙壁上的铭文对理解神庙的文化职能至关重要，应立即破译。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

A heavy stone doorway was discovered blocking off the deepest part of the temple. After breaking through, excavators found a circular chamber with a central pit opening directly above a natural underground magma reservoir. The orange light of the magma bathed the walls in a red glow, faintly illuminating inscriptions encircling the strange room. The top of the surrounding walls are studded with bright, flaming eyes.\n\nOn one edge of this pit are the desiccated remains of one of the locals, broken open upon an altar shaped like the Infernal Eye. It appears they were in fact sacrificing their own in an attempt to appease it.\n\nThe text along these walls may be critical to understanding the cultural role of this temple, and should be translated immediately.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:623`

## infernals.2735.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2735.name&lang=en)

### 中文

剧变预言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

Convulsing Prophecies

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:624`

## infernals.2735.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.2735.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最终研究发现，神庙最深处房间中的经文揭示了一系列围绕地狱之眼回归的预言，讲述地狱之眼因臣民蔑视燃起怒火，将使天空变暗、圣山喷发、特定作物枯萎，灾祸连绵不断。预言预示文明终结，唯有服从地狱之眼者得以幸存。\n\n本地特工探寻其中的文化关联，发现此预言是该行星文化中许多谚语和神话的源头，而当地现代神庙所举行的神秘仪式则是对此古老预言的重新演绎或反抗。\n\n我方技术可以重现预言中的事件，或通过伪造，以假乱真。倘若我们自诩地狱之眼转世，印证预言不虚，便能迫使对方让步并效忠于我们，为长期同化创造条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

The final study of the scripture in the temple's innermost chamber reveals a set of prophecies relating to the return of the Infernal Eye. It speaks of the Infernal Eye growing angry at the defiance of its subjects. In response, the Eye is expected to darken the skies, erupt a specific holy mountain, cause a specific crop to wither on the vine, and more. The prophecy presents this as the end of civilization, stating that after these events, only those who submit to the Infernal Eye shall survive. \n\nOur agents among the local populace have looked for cultural connections, and found that this prophetic text is the source of many aphorisms and phrases in the planet's cultures, as well as the source text for many later stories and myths. They also believe that the mysteries practiced by their modern temples include variations on, and reactions against, this ancient prophecy. \n\nAll of these prophesied events could be re-enacted with our technology, or faked with a degree of realism they will not be able to detect. If we were to present ourselves as the Infernal Eye reincarnate, demonstrating that the original prophecy was right all along, we may be able to extract concessions and fealty from them. This would make them easier to absorb in the long term.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:625`

## infernals.35.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernals.35.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[from.GetName]§!地表崩裂，地壳破碎，难以承受的热浪从地底涌上。转变来得迅猛而彻底：群山崩溃，海洋沸腾，天空余烬弥漫。\n\n曾经繁盛的生物群系已不复存在，唯有极端生命体仍栖息于此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:580`

### English

The surface of §H[from.GetName]§! has ruptured, its crust fracturing as unbearable heat surged from below. The transformation was swift and absolute: mountains collapsed, oceans boiled, and the sky was a veil of embers.\n\nNo trace remains of the planet's once-thriving biomes. Only extremophile life forms can still call this place home.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:640`

## infernal_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernal_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些飞船发出明亮炽热的火焰，驱散了宇宙中的冰冷黑暗。它们坚韧的装甲几乎无法遏制其制造者称为母星的星球之火。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:615`

### English

The bright, molten heat of these ships burns away the cold darkness of space itself. Their tough panels barely contain the fire of the worlds their makers call home.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:676`

## decision_living_metal_distribution_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_living_metal_distribution_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$r_living_metal$融入我们基础设施的脉络，使日常设施不停歇地高效运转：建筑自行修复，运输网络实时调整，工具与有机体之间的界限变得模糊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/infernals_l_simp_chinese.yml:617`

### English

Infused into the arteries of our infrastructure, $r_living_metal$ breathes a restless efficiency into the machinery of daily life. From self-mending structures to transit systems that adapt in real-time, its integration blurs the line between tool and organism.

Source: `localisation/english/infernals_l_english.yml:678`
